reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:413: trailing whitespace.
+**Question.** A cone meets a cylinder, say with its axis 10 degrees off perpendicular. How can the
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:414: trailing whitespace.
+intersection engine become general enough to handle that, and what else would the same change buy?
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:415: trailing whitespace.
+
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:416: trailing whitespace.
+## Answer in brief
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:417: trailing whitespace.
+
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:418: trailing whitespace.
+1. **Today the pair is refused.** `plan_intersections` stops at the first cone face with
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:419: trailing whitespace.
+   `face N has no qualified analytic or bilinear support`. The legacy certified engine
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:420: trailing whitespace.
+   (`query_intersection`) classifies the pair but is not a usable route (section 1).
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:421: trailing whitespace.
+2. **The structure generalizes cleanly.** Plane, Cylinder and Cone are all quadrics, and Cylinder and
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:422: trailing whitespace.
+   Cone are ruled: `S(t, s) = P(t) + s D(t)`. Along a ruling a second quadric gives
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:423: trailing whitespace.
+   `A(t) s^2 + B(t) s + C(t) = 0`. That is exactly the structure `CylinderIntersectionCurve` already
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:424: trailing whitespace.
+   uses, with a degree-8 tangent-half polynomial for the events. A prototype reproduces the production
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:425: trailing whitespace.
+   cylinder charts and extends to cones with verified completeness and accuracy.
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:426: trailing whitespace.
+3. **The chart side is small.** Letting an arrangement domain be a Cone face is about 20 changed lines
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:427: trailing whitespace.
+   in `material_arrangement.py`; the arrangement itself is chart-agnostic.
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:428: trailing whitespace.
+4. **The curve must stay analytic.** The same arrangement is 225 to 700 times slower when the exact
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:429: trailing whitespace.
+   curve is replaced by a fitted Bezier chain. A numeric or Bezier fallback is not a route to
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:430: trailing whitespace.
+   generality inside this engine.
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:431: trailing whitespace.
+5. **Recommendation.** Add a generalized quadric branch curve as a new family beside
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:432: trailing whitespace.
+   `CylinderIntersectionCurve` (leave the validated cylinder code alone), starting with
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:433: trailing whitespace.
+   cone x cylinder and cone x plane. Decisions that belong to the owner are in section 6.
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:434: trailing whitespace.
+
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:435: trailing whitespace.

+## 2. The structural idea
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:460: trailing whitespace.
+
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:461: trailing whitespace.
+For a ruled first support and any implicit quadric `x.M.x + 2 l.x + c = 0`:
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:462: trailing whitespace.
+
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:463: trailing whitespace.
+* `A = D.M.D`, `B = 2 (P.M.D + l.D)`, `C = P.M.P + 2 l.P + c` are trigonometric polynomials of degree
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:464: trailing whitespace.
+  at most 2, built exactly from the doubles (Cylinder: constant `D`; Cone: constant `P`, the apex).
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:465: trailing whitespace.
+* The branches are `s = (-B +- sqrt(B^2 - 4AC)) / (2A)`. Folds are roots of the degree-4 discriminant
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:466: trailing whitespace.
+  (odd multiplicity), double contacts are even multiplicity, poles are roots of `A`, and finite-patch
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:467: trailing whitespace.
+  boundaries are roots of `A s0^2 + B s0 + C` and of resultants against the other support's end and seam
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:468: trailing whitespace.
+  planes. All are Sturm-isolated with the existing `isolate_real_roots`.
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:469: trailing whitespace.
+* Curve events against any other quadric are the resultant of two such quadratics: again a degree-8
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:470: trailing whitespace.
+  tangent-half polynomial for Cylinder and Cone supports.
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:471: trailing whitespace.
+* Sphere, ellipsoid and similar quadrics are free **as the second support**. The ruled partner supplies
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:472: trailing whitespace.
+  the angle. A torus is quartic and is not covered.
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:473: trailing whitespace.
+
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:474: trailing whitespace.
+Choice of the angle-supplying support matters for regularity. For the 10 degree case cone-first has one
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:475: trailing whitespace.
+smooth closed loop (four charts, cut only at seam events; no folds, no poles); cylinder-first folds twice
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:476: trailing whitespace.
+per loop (eight charts, four folds). The persisted curve should use whichever has fewer folds.
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:477: trailing whitespace.
+
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:478: trailing whitespace.

The singular equal-radius perpendicular case is included. |
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:487: trailing whitespace.
+| Complete | An independent grid oracle (contour of the implicit equation on a dense chart, no use of A, B, C or events) over 6 pairings x 150 random configurations: about 247,000 contour points, 1,700 charts, 831 folds, 88 poles, none uncovered away from fold angles. Pairings: cone/cylinder both orders, cone/cone, cone/plane, cylinder/plane, cylinder/cylinder. |

reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:492: trailing whitespace.
+| Cone as a material chart | `MaterialDomain` accepts Cone faces with about 20 changed lines. Generator, ring and combined cuts on upright, 10 degree and generic-tilt cone facets give the exact product areas. |
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:493: trailing whitespace.
+| Curve class matters | Same arrangement, same cylinder x cylinder facets, same tolerances: analytic traces 0.09 to 0.22 s per face; degree-5 Bezier fits (1e-9) 25 to 120+ s per face, 225x to 700x slower (one case was cancelled at the 120 s budget). A Bezier-trace cone x cylinder arrangement at 10 degrees did succeed on all 8 cone facets (two cells each, area conserved) but took 28 to 44 s per facet. |
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:494: trailing whitespace.
+
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:495: trailing whitespace.
+Fail-closed behavior in the prototype (typed `GeometryError`, never a guess): cone apex on the other
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:496: trailing whitespace.
+support within tolerance (the exact structure then depends on rounding of the apex position), contact
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:497: trailing whitespace.
+of multiplicity three or more, and rulings parallel to a plane for every angle.
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:498: trailing whitespace.

reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:499: trailing whitespace.
+## 4. Options
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:500: trailing whitespace.
+
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:501: trailing whitespace.
+* **A. Numeric or Bezier fallback inside the batch engine.** Rejected by the data above: the legacy
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:502: trailing whitespace.
+  provider is fragmentary and persists 1e-8 fits, and Bezier curves make the exact arrangement two to
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:503: trailing whitespace.
+  three orders of magnitude slower.
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:504: trailing whitespace.
+* **B. Exact generalized quadric branch (recommended).** Covers Cone now and Sphere or any quadric as a
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:505: trailing whitespace.
+  second support later. Cost: a new curve class, a geometry schema bump (6), junction dispatch, chart and
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:506: trailing whitespace.
+  `_child_support` work, qualification, and consumer coordination.
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:507: trailing whitespace.
+* **C. Revolution and freeform surfaces (torus, profiles, Ruled/Coons/NURBS).** Needs certified numeric
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:508: trailing whitespace.
+  surface-surface tracing and an arrangement that can use numeric curves without exact resultants. A
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:509: trailing whitespace.
+  separate project; B does not block it.
reports/general_intersections/quadric_generalization_study.md:510: trailing whitespace.
+* **D. Architecture.** Define a small curve protocol (evaluate, derivative, certified bounds, subcurve,
